1	// Acceptance tests for the movement-and-rotation Gherkin feature.
2	// Run separately from unit tests: go test ./acceptance/...
3	//
4	// When GHERKIN_FEATURE_JSON is set, example values are loaded from that file,
5	// enabling gherkin-mutator to drive differential mutation.
6	package movement_and_rotation_test
7	
8	import (
9		"encoding/json"
10		"math/rand"
11		"os"
12		"strconv"
13		"testing"
14	
15		"github.com/gamaleds/tetris/pkg/game"
16	)
17	
18	// --- Feature JSON loader ---
19	
20	type gherkinFeature struct {
21		Scenarios []gherkinScenario `json:"scenarios"`
22	}
23	
24	type gherkinScenario struct {
25		Examples []map[string]string `json:"examples"`
26	}
27	
28	var loadedFeature *gherkinFeature
29	
30	func featureExamples(scenarioIndex, exampleIndex int) map[string]string {
31		if loadedFeature == nil {
32			path := os.Getenv("GHERKIN_FEATURE_JSON")
33			if path == "" {
34				return nil
35			}
36			f, err := os.Open(path)
37			if err != nil {
38				return nil
39			}
40			defer f.Close()
41			var feat gherkinFeature
42			if err := json.NewDecoder(f).Decode(&feat); err != nil {
43				return nil
44			}
45			loadedFeature = &feat
46		}
47		if scenarioIndex >= len(loadedFeature.Scenarios) {
48			return nil
49		}
50		s := loadedFeature.Scenarios[scenarioIndex]
51		if exampleIndex >= len(s.Examples) {
52			return nil
53		}
54		return s.Examples[exampleIndex]
55	}
56	
57	func exStr(scenarioIndex, exampleIndex int, key, def string) string {
58		ex := featureExamples(scenarioIndex, exampleIndex)
59		if v, ok := ex[key]; ok {
60			return v
61		}
62		return def
63	}
64	
65	func exInt(scenarioIndex, exampleIndex int, key string, def int) int {
66		v := exStr(scenarioIndex, exampleIndex, key, "")
67		if v == "" {
68			return def
69		}
70		i, err := strconv.Atoi(v)
71		if err != nil {
72			return def
73		}
74		return i
75	}
76	
77	func exBool(scenarioIndex, exampleIndex int, key string, def bool) bool {
78		v := exStr(scenarioIndex, exampleIndex, key, "")
79		if v == "" {
80			return def
81		}
82		return v == "true"
83	}
84	
85	// --- Helpers ---
86	
87	func sessionInPhase(t *testing.T, phase string) *game.Session {
88		t.Helper()
89		s := game.NewSession(rand.New(rand.NewSource(42)))
90		switch phase {
91		case "playing":
92			s.PlayerStartsGame()
93		case "paused":
94			s.PlayerStartsGame()
95			s.PlayerPauses()
96		default:
97			t.Fatalf("unknown phase: %s", phase)
98		}
99		return s
100	}
101	
102	func rotationFromString(r string) game.Rotation {
103		switch r {
104		case "north":
105			return game.RotationNorth
106		case "east":
107			return game.RotationEast
108		case "south":
109			return game.RotationSouth
110		case "west":
111			return game.RotationWest
112		}
113		return game.RotationNorth
114	}
115	
116	// setActivePieceAtCol sets up an O-piece at the given origin col.
117	// O-piece north offsets: {1,0},{2,0},{1,1},{2,1} — occupies cols origin.col+1 and origin.col+2.
118	// canMoveLeft=false: place blocker at col+0 (the column the left-shifted piece would enter).
119	// canMoveRight=false: place blocker at col+3 (the column the right-shifted piece would enter).
120	func setActivePieceAtCol(s *game.Session, col int, canMoveLeft, canMoveRight bool) {
121		s.ActivePiece = &game.ActivePiece{Kind: game.KindO, Origin: game.Position{Col: col, Row: 5}, Rotation: game.RotationNorth}
122		if !canMoveLeft {
123			// After moving left, origin.col-1 piece occupies cols col and col+1.
124			// Block col+0 to prevent entry.
125			s.Board.SetCell(col, 5, game.KindI)
126			s.Board.SetCell(col, 6, game.KindI)
127		}
128		if !canMoveRight {
129			// After moving right, origin.col+1 piece occupies cols col+2 and col+3.
130			// Block col+3 to prevent entry.
131			blocker := col + 3
132			if blocker < game.BoardCols {
133				s.Board.SetCell(blocker, 5, game.KindI)
134				s.Board.SetCell(blocker, 6, game.KindI)
135			}
136		}
137	}
138	
139	// setActivePieceWithRotation sets up an active piece at a clear mid-board position.
140	// canRotateCW=false: surround with blockers on all 4 sides so CW rotation fails.
141	// canRotateCCW=false: surround with blockers so CCW rotation fails.
142	func setActivePieceWithRotation(s *game.Session, rotStr string, canRotateCW, canRotateCCW bool) {
143		rot := rotationFromString(rotStr)
144		// Place T-piece in middle of board where SRS kicks have room
145		s.ActivePiece = &game.ActivePiece{Kind: game.KindT, Origin: game.Position{Col: 4, Row: 8}, Rotation: rot}
146	
147		if !canRotateCW && !canRotateCCW {
148			// Block all surrounding cells so all 5 kick candidates fail for both directions
149			for dc := -3; dc <= 4; dc++ {
150				for dr := -3; dr <= 4; dr++ {
151					c := 4 + dc
152					r := 8 + dr
153					if c >= 0 && c < game.BoardCols && r >= 0 && r < game.BoardRows {
154						s.Board.SetCell(c, r, game.KindI)
155					}
156				}
157			}
158			s.ActivePiece.Origin = game.Position{Col: 4, Row: 8}
159		} else if !canRotateCW {
160			// For the "blocked CW" scenario from the feature: use O-piece which has same
161			// footprint in every rotation — any placement of O "rotates" (footprint unchanged)
162			// so we need a piece that genuinely blocks. Use a fully-blocked T-piece.
163			// Simplest: fill enough cells that the CW kick candidates all fail.
164			// T north→east: CW rotation tries offsets {0,0},{-1,0},{-1,-1},{0,2},{-1,2}.
165			// We need all 5 to be invalid. Place walls.
166			for dc := -2; dc <= 3; dc++ {
167				for dr := -2; dr <= 3; dr++ {
168					if dc == 0 && dr >= 0 && dr <= 1 {
169						continue // keep current footprint cells free (already placed)
170					}
171					c := 4 + dc
172					r := 8 + dr
173					if c >= 0 && c < game.BoardCols && r >= 0 && r < game.BoardRows {
174						s.Board.SetCell(c, r, game.KindI)
175					}
176				}
177			}
178			s.ActivePiece.Origin = game.Position{Col: 4, Row: 8}
179		} else if !canRotateCCW {
180			// Similar: block all CCW kick candidates
181			for dc := -2; dc <= 3; dc++ {
182				for dr := -2; dr <= 3; dr++ {
183					if dc == 0 && dr >= 0 && dr <= 1 {
184						continue
185					}
186					c := 4 + dc
187					r := 8 + dr
188					if c >= 0 && c < game.BoardCols && r >= 0 && r < game.BoardRows {
189						s.Board.SetCell(c, r, game.KindI)
190					}
191				}
192			}
193			s.ActivePiece.Origin = game.Position{Col: 4, Row: 8}
194		}
195	}
196	
197	// --- Scenario movement_and_rotation-1: Moving left shifts col by -1 ---
198	// Scenario index 0, examples index 0..1
199	
200	func TestACC_MovementAndRotation1_MoveLeftShiftsCol(t *testing.T) {
201		defaults := []struct{ col, newCol int }{{5, 4}, {1, 0}}
202		for i, def := range defaults {
203			col := exInt(0, i, "col", def.col)
204			newCol := exInt(0, i, "new_col", def.newCol)
205			t.Run(strconv.Itoa(col), func(t *testing.T) {
206				s := sessionInPhase(t, "playing")
207				setActivePieceAtCol(s, col, true, true)
208				if !s.ActivePiece.CanMoveLeft(s.Board) {
209					t.Errorf("board setup: piece at col %d cannot move left", col)
210					return
211				}
212				s.PlayerMovesLeft()
213				if s.ActivePiece.Origin.Col != newCol {
214					t.Errorf("col %d: after move left origin.col = %d, want %d", col, s.ActivePiece.Origin.Col, newCol)
215				}
216			})
217		}
218	}
219	
220	// --- Scenario movement_and_rotation-2: Move left blocked when can_move_left false ---
221	// Scenario index 1, no examples
222	
223	func TestACC_MovementAndRotation2_MoveLeftBlocked(t *testing.T) {
224		s := sessionInPhase(t, "playing")
225		setActivePieceAtCol(s, 0, false, true)
226		s.PlayerMovesLeft()
227		if s.ActivePiece.Origin.Col != 0 {
228			t.Errorf("col = %d after blocked move left, want 0", s.ActivePiece.Origin.Col)
229		}
230	}
231	
232	// --- Scenario movement_and_rotation-3: Moving right shifts col by +1 ---
233	// Scenario index 2, examples index 0..1
234	
235	func TestACC_MovementAndRotation3_MoveRightShiftsCol(t *testing.T) {
236		// Default col values use T-piece north (max offset +2), so col+3 must fit in board (≤9).
237		defaults := []struct{ col, newCol int }{{3, 4}, {5, 6}}
238		for i, def := range defaults {
239			col := exInt(2, i, "col", def.col)
240			newCol := exInt(2, i, "new_col", def.newCol)
241			t.Run(strconv.Itoa(col), func(t *testing.T) {
242				s := sessionInPhase(t, "playing")
243				s.ActivePiece = &game.ActivePiece{Kind: game.KindT, Origin: game.Position{Col: col, Row: 5}, Rotation: game.RotationNorth}
244				if !s.ActivePiece.CanMoveRight(s.Board) {
245					t.Errorf("board setup: piece at col %d cannot move right", col)
246					return
247				}
248				s.PlayerMovesRight()
249				if s.ActivePiece.Origin.Col != newCol {
250					t.Errorf("col %d: after move right origin.col = %d, want %d", col, s.ActivePiece.Origin.Col, newCol)
251				}
252			})
253		}
254	}
255	
256	// --- Scenario movement_and_rotation-4: Move right blocked ---
257	// Scenario index 3, no examples
258	
259	// I-piece east (offset +2 col) at origin col=7 places the piece at the right edge (col 9).
260	// Moving right would reach col 10 — out of bounds, so CanMoveRight=false.
261	func TestACC_MovementAndRotation4_MoveRightBlocked(t *testing.T) {
262		s := sessionInPhase(t, "playing")
263		s.ActivePiece = &game.ActivePiece{Kind: game.KindI, Origin: game.Position{Col: 7, Row: 5}, Rotation: game.RotationEast}
264		if s.ActivePiece.CanMoveRight(s.Board) {
265			t.Fatal("board setup: expected piece to be blocked on right, but CanMoveRight=true")
266		}
267		startCol := s.ActivePiece.Origin.Col
268		s.PlayerMovesRight()
269		if s.ActivePiece.Origin.Col != startCol {
270			t.Errorf("col = %d after blocked move right, want %d", s.ActivePiece.Origin.Col, startCol)
271		}
272	}
273	
274	// --- Scenario movement_and_rotation-5: Move left resets lock countdown when at rest ---
275	// Scenario index 4, examples index 0..1
276	
277	func TestACC_MovementAndRotation5_MoveLeftResetsLock(t *testing.T) {
278		defaults := []struct{ before int }{{15}, {1}}
279		for i, def := range defaults {
280			before := exInt(4, i, "before", def.before)
281			t.Run(strconv.Itoa(before), func(t *testing.T) {
282				s := sessionInPhase(t, "playing")
283				// Place O-piece at rest at col 5 (ghost row)
284				ap := &game.ActivePiece{Kind: game.KindO, Origin: game.Position{Col: 5, Row: 0}, Rotation: game.RotationNorth}
285				ghost := ap.GhostOrigin(s.Board)
286				s.ActivePiece = &game.ActivePiece{Kind: game.KindO, Origin: ghost, Rotation: game.RotationNorth}
287				if !s.ActivePiece.AtRest(s.Board) {
288					t.Fatal("board setup: piece is not at rest")
289				}
290				if !s.ActivePiece.CanMoveLeft(s.Board) {
291					t.Fatal("board setup: piece cannot move left")
292				}
293				s.LockTicksRemaining = before
294				s.PlayerMovesLeft()
295				if s.LockTicksRemaining != game.LockTicks {
296					t.Errorf("before=%d: lock_ticks_remaining = %d, want %d", before, s.LockTicksRemaining, game.LockTicks)
297				}
298			})
299		}
300	}
301

345	// --- Scenario movement_and_rotation-8: Rotate CW updates rotation ---

348	func TestACC_MovementAndRotation8_RotateCWUpdatesRotation(t *testing.T) {
349		s := sessionInPhase(t, "playing")
350		s.ActivePiece = &game.ActivePiece{Kind: game.KindT, Origin: game.Position{Col: 4, Row: 5}, Rotation: game.RotationNorth}
351		if !s.ActivePiece.CanRotateCW(s.Board) {

360	// --- Scenario movement_and_rotation-9: Rotate CCW updates rotation ---
361	// Scenario index 8, no examples
362	
363	func TestACC_MovementAndRotation9_RotateCCWUpdatesRotation(t *testing.T) {
364		s := sessionInPhase(t, "playing")
365		s.ActivePiece = &game.ActivePiece{Kind: game.KindT, Origin: game.Position{Col: 4, Row: 5}, Rotation: game.RotationNorth}
366		if !s.ActivePiece.CanRotateCCW(s.Board) {
367			t.Fatal("board setup: piece cannot rotate CCW")

371			t.Errorf("rotation = %v, want west", s.ActivePiece.Rotation)
372		}

399	// --- Scenario movement_and_rotation-12: Rotating CW resets lock when at rest ---
400	// Scenario index 11, examples index 0..1
401	
402	func TestACC_MovementAndRotation12_RotateCWResetsLock(t *testing.T) {
403		defaults := []struct {
404			rotation string
405			before   int
406		}{{"north", 15}, {"east", 1}}
407		for i, def := range defaults {
408			rotStr := exStr(11, i, "rotation", def.rotation)
409			before := exInt(11, i, "before", def.before)
410			t.Run(rotStr+"_"+strconv.Itoa(before), func(t *testing.T) {
411				s := sessionInPhase(t, "playing")
412				rot := rotationFromString(rotStr)
413				// Place T at bottom — at rest
414				s.ActivePiece = &game.ActivePiece{Kind: game.KindT, Origin: game.Position{Col: 4, Row: 18}, Rotation: rot}
415				if !s.ActivePiece.AtRest(s.Board) {
416					t.Skip("piece not at rest — board setup not applicable")
417				}
418				if !s.ActivePiece.CanRotateCW(s.Board) {
419					t.Skip("piece cannot rotate CW from this position")
420				}
421				s.LockTicksRemaining = before
422				s.PlayerRotatesCW()
423				if s.ActivePiece.AtRest(s.Board) && s.LockTicksRemaining != game.LockTicks {
424					t.Errorf("before=%d: lock_ticks_remaining = %d, want %d", before, s.LockTicksRemaining, game.LockTicks)
425				}
426			})
427		}
428	}

  "new_string": "func setActivePieceAtCol(s *game.Session, col int, canMoveLeft, canMoveRight bool) {\n\ts.SetActivePieceForTest(game.KindO, col, 5, game.RotationNorth)\n\tif !canMoveLeft {\n\t\ts.SetBoardCellForTest(col, 5, game.KindI)\n\t\ts.SetBoardCellForTest(col, 6, game.KindI)\n\t}\n\tif !canMoveRight {\n\t\tblocker := col + 3\n\t\tif blocker < game.BoardCols {\n\t\t\ts.SetBoardCellForTest(blocker, 5, game.KindI)\n\t\t\ts.SetBoardCellForTest(blocker, 6, game.KindI)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}"
}

Use a fully-blocked T-piece.\n\t\t// Simplest: fill enough cells that the CW kick candidates all fail.\n\t\t// T north→east: CW rotation tries offsets {0,0},{-1,0},{-1,-1},{0,2},{-1,2}.\n\t\t// We need all 5 to be invalid. Place walls.\n\t\tfor dc := -2; dc <= 3; dc++ {\n\t\t\tfor dr := -2; dr <= 3; dr++ {\n\t\t\t\tif dc == 0 && dr >= 0 && dr <= 1 {\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue // keep current footprint cells free (already placed)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tc := 4 + dc\n\t\t\t\tr := 8 + dr\n\t\t\t\tif c >= 0 && c < game.BoardCols && r >= 0 && r < game.BoardRows {\n\t\t\t\t\ts.Board.SetCell(c, r, game.KindI)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\ts.ActivePiece.Origin = game.Position{Col: 4, Row: 8}\n\t} else if !canRotateCCW {\n\t\t// Similar: block all CCW kick candidates\n\t\tfor dc := -2; dc <= 3; dc++ {\n\t\t\tfor dr := -2; dr <= 3; dr++ {\n\t\t\t\tif dc == 0 && dr >= 0 && dr <= 1 {\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tc := 4 + dc\n\t\t\t\tr := 8 + dr\n\t\t\t\tif c >= 0 && c < game.BoardCols && r >= 0 && r < game.BoardRows {\n\t\t\t\t\ts.Board.SetCell(c, r, game.KindI)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\ts.ActivePiece.Origin = game.Position{Col: 4, Row: 8}\n\t}\n}",

1	// Acceptance tests for the soft-drop Gherkin feature.
2	// Run separately from unit tests: go test ./acceptance/...
3	//
4	// When GHERKIN_FEATURE_JSON is set, example values are loaded from that file,
5	// enabling gherkin-mutator to drive differential mutation.
6	package soft_drop_test
7	
8	import (
9		"encoding/json"
10		"math/rand"
11		"os"
12		"strconv"
13		"testing"
14	
15		"github.com/gamaleds/tetris/pkg/game"
16	)
17	
18	// --- Feature JSON loader ---
19	
20	type gherkinFeature struct {
21		Scenarios []gherkinScenario `json:"scenarios"`
22	}
23	
24	type gherkinScenario struct {
25		Examples []map[string]string `json:"examples"`
26	}
27	
28	var loadedFeature *gherkinFeature
29	
30	func featureExamples(scenarioIndex, exampleIndex int) map[string]string {
31		if loadedFeature == nil {
32			path := os.Getenv("GHERKIN_FEATURE_JSON")
33			if path == "" {
34				return nil
35			}
36			f, err := os.Open(path)
37			if err != nil {
38				return nil
39			}
40			defer f.Close()
41			var feat gherkinFeature
42			if err := json.NewDecoder(f).Decode(&feat); err != nil {
43				return nil
44			}
45			loadedFeature = &feat
46		}
47		if scenarioIndex >= len(loadedFeature.Scenarios) {
48			return nil
49		}
50		s := loadedFeature.Scenarios[scenarioIndex]
51		if exampleIndex >= len(s.Examples) {
52			return nil
53		}
54		return s.Examples[exampleIndex]
55	}
56	
57	func exStr(scenarioIndex, exampleIndex int, key, def string) string {
58		ex := featureExamples(scenarioIndex, exampleIndex)
59		if v, ok := ex[key]; ok {
60			return v
61		}
62		return def
63	}
64	
65	func exInt(scenarioIndex, exampleIndex int, key string, def int) int {
66		v := exStr(scenarioIndex, exampleIndex, key, "")
67		if v == "" {
68			return def
69		}
70		i, err := strconv.Atoi(v)
71		if err != nil {
72			return def
73		}
74		return i
75	}
76	
77	// --- Helpers ---
78	
79	func sessionInPhase(t *testing.T, phase string) *game.Session {
80		t.Helper()
81		s := game.NewSession(rand.New(rand.NewSource(42)))
82		switch phase {
83		case "playing":
84			s.PlayerStartsGame()
85		case "paused":
86			s.PlayerStartsGame()
87			s.PlayerPauses()
88		default:
89			t.Fatalf("unknown phase: %s", phase)
90		}
91		return s
92	}
93	
94	// pieceNotAtRest places an O-piece at the given origin row and asserts it is not at rest.
95	// The O-piece (2×2) is safe: on an empty board it is not at rest until its bottom reaches row 19.
96	func pieceNotAtRest(t *testing.T, s *game.Session, row int) {
97		t.Helper()
98		s.ActivePiece = &game.ActivePiece{
99			Kind:     game.KindO,
100			Origin:   game.Position{Col: 4, Row: row},
101			Rotation: game.RotationNorth,
102		}
103		if s.ActivePiece.AtRest(s.Board) {
104			t.Errorf("board setup: O-piece at row %d is already at rest — example not applicable", row)
105			return
106		}
107	}
108	
109	// pieceAtRest places an O-piece at the ghost position from row 0 (the floor) so AtRest is true.
110	// The ghost is determined by the board; on an empty board the O-piece rests at row 18.
111	func pieceAtRest(t *testing.T, s *game.Session, wantRow int) {
112		t.Helper()
113		ap := &game.ActivePiece{
114			Kind:     game.KindO,
115			Origin:   game.Position{Col: 4, Row: 0},
116			Rotation: game.RotationNorth,
117		}
118		ghost := ap.GhostOrigin(s.Board)
119		if ghost.Row != wantRow {
120			t.Errorf("board setup: O-piece ghost row = %d, want %d", ghost.Row, wantRow)
121			return
122		}
123		s.ActivePiece = &game.ActivePiece{
124			Kind:     game.KindO,
125			Origin:   ghost,
126			Rotation: game.RotationNorth,
127		}
128		if !s.ActivePiece.AtRest(s.Board) {
129			t.Errorf("board setup: O-piece at ghost row %d is not at rest", ghost.Row)
130			return
131		}
132	}
133	
134	// --- Scenario soft_drop-1: Soft drop moves the active piece one row down ---
135	// Scenario index 0, examples index 0..1
136	
137	func TestACC_SoftDrop1_MovesOneRowDown(t *testing.T) {
138		defaults := []struct{ row, newRow int }{{5, 6}, {0, 1}}
139		for i, def := range defaults {
140			row := exInt(0, i, "row", def.row)
141			newRow := exInt(0, i, "new_row", def.newRow)
142			t.Run(strconv.Itoa(row), func(t *testing.T) {
143				s := sessionInPhase(t, "playing")
144				pieceNotAtRest(t, s, row)
145				if t.Failed() {
146					return
147				}
148				ok := s.PlayerSoftDrops()
149				if !ok {
150					t.Fatalf("PlayerSoftDrops returned false for row %d (expected accepted)", row)
151				}
152				if s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row != newRow {
153					t.Errorf("row %d: after soft drop origin.row = %d, want %d", row, s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row, newRow)
154				}
155			})
156		}
157	}
158	
159	// --- Scenario soft_drop-2: Soft drop awards one point per row ---
160	// Scenario index 1, examples index 0..1
161	
162	func TestACC_SoftDrop2_AwardsOnePoint(t *testing.T) {
163		defaults := []struct{ before, after int }{{0, 1}, {40, 41}}
164		for i, def := range defaults {
165			before := exInt(1, i, "before", def.before)
166			after := exInt(1, i, "after", def.after)
167			t.Run(strconv.Itoa(before), func(t *testing.T) {
168				s := sessionInPhase(t, "playing")
169				// Scenario specifies row 5 (fixed), not at rest.
170				pieceNotAtRest(t, s, 5)
171				if t.Failed() {
172					return
173				}
174				s.Score = before
175				ok := s.PlayerSoftDrops()
176				if !ok {
177					t.Fatalf("PlayerSoftDrops returned false (score_before=%d)", before)
178				}
179				if s.Score != after {
180					t.Errorf("score_before=%d: score = %d, want %d", before, s.Score, after)
181				}
182			})
183		}
184	}
185	
186	// --- Scenario soft_drop-3: Soft drop is blocked when the piece is at rest ---
187	// Scenario index 2, no examples
188	
189	func TestACC_SoftDrop3_BlockedAtRest(t *testing.T) {
190		s := sessionInPhase(t, "playing")
191		// O-piece on empty board rests at row 18 (2-row piece, bottom at row 19).
192		pieceAtRest(t, s, 18)
193		if t.Failed() {
194			return
195		}
196		s.Score = 7
197		ok := s.PlayerSoftDrops()
198		if ok {
199			t.Error("PlayerSoftDrops returned true when piece is at rest")
200		}
201		if s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row != 18 {
202			t.Errorf("piece moved while at rest: row = %d, want 18", s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row)
203		}
204		if s.Score != 7 {
205			t.Errorf("score changed while blocked: score = %d, want 7", s.Score)
206		}
207	}
208	
209	// --- Scenario soft_drop-4: Soft drop is ignored when phase is not playing ---
210	// Scenario index 3, no examples
211	
212	func TestACC_SoftDrop4_IgnoredNotPlaying(t *testing.T) {
213		s := sessionInPhase(t, "paused")
214		s.ActivePiece = &game.ActivePiece{
215			Kind:     game.KindO,
216			Origin:   game.Position{Col: 4, Row: 5},
217			Rotation: game.RotationNorth,
218		}
219		s.Score = 3
220		ok := s.PlayerSoftDrops()
221		if ok {
222			t.Error("PlayerSoftDrops returned true when phase is paused")
223		}
224		if s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row != 5 {
225			t.Errorf("piece moved in paused phase: row = %d, want 5", s.ActivePiece.Origin.Row)
226		}
227		if s.Score != 3 {
228			t.Errorf("score changed in paused phase: score = %d, want 3", s.Score)
229		}
230	}

1	// Acceptance tests for the lines-and-score Gherkin feature.
2	// Run separately from unit tests: go test ./acceptance/...
3	//
4	// When GHERKIN_FEATURE_JSON is set, example values are loaded from that file,
5	// enabling gherkin-mutator to drive differential mutation.
6	package lines_and_score_test
7	
8	import (
9		"encoding/json"
10		"math/rand"
11		"os"
12		"strconv"
13		"testing"
14	
15		"github.com/gamaleds/tetris/pkg/game"
16	)
17	
18	// --- Feature JSON loader ---
19	
20	type gherkinFeature struct {
21		Scenarios []gherkinScenario `json:"scenarios"`
22	}
23	
24	type gherkinScenario struct {
25		Examples []map[string]string `json:"examples"`
26	}
27	
28	var loadedFeature *gherkinFeature
29	
30	func featureExamples(scenarioIndex, exampleIndex int) map[string]string {
31		if loadedFeature == nil {
32			path := os.Getenv("GHERKIN_FEATURE_JSON")
33			if path == "" {
34				return nil
35			}
36			f, err := os.Open(path)
37			if err != nil {
38				return nil
39			}
40			defer f.Close()
41			var feat gherkinFeature
42			if err := json.NewDecoder(f).Decode(&feat); err != nil {
43				return nil
44			}
45			loadedFeature = &feat
46		}
47		if scenarioIndex >= len(loadedFeature.Scenarios) {
48			return nil
49		}
50		s := loadedFeature.Scenarios[scenarioIndex]
51		if exampleIndex >= len(s.Examples) {
52			return nil
53		}
54		return s.Examples[exampleIndex]
55	}
56	
57	func exStr(scenarioIndex, exampleIndex int, key, def string) string {
58		ex := featureExamples(scenarioIndex, exampleIndex)
59		if v, ok := ex[key]; ok {
60			return v
61		}
62		return def
63	}
64	
65	func exInt(scenarioIndex, exampleIndex int, key string, def int) int {
66		v := exStr(scenarioIndex, exampleIndex, key, "")
67		if v == "" {
68			return def
69		}
70		i, err := strconv.Atoi(v)
71		if err != nil {
72			return def
73		}
74		return i
75	}
76	
77	// --- Helpers ---
78	
79	// sessionSetup creates a playing session with the given level and linesClearedTotal.
80	func sessionSetup(t *testing.T, level, linesBefore int) *game.Session {
81		t.Helper()
82		s := game.NewSession(rand.New(rand.NewSource(42)))
83		s.PlayerStartsGame()
84		s.Level = level
85		s.LinesClearedTotal = linesBefore
86		return s
87	}
88	
89	// fillRows fills the bottom `count` visible rows completely with KindI cells.
90	// Returns the row indices that were filled (from bottom up).
91	func fillRows(b *game.Board, count int) []int {
92		filled := make([]int, count)
93		for i := 0; i < count; i++ {
94			row := game.BoardRows - 1 - i
95			filled[i] = row
96			for c := 0; c < game.BoardCols; c++ {
97				b.SetCell(c, row, game.KindI)
98			}
99		}
100		return filled
101	}
102	
103	// setupLockWithFullRows places an O-piece at rest above the pre-filled rows and
104	// sets LockTicksRemaining=1. Calling Tick() will lock the piece and trigger linesCleared.
105	// The O-piece (2×2) sits at rows (topRow, topRow+1); the pre-filled rows start at (topRow+2).
106	func setupLockWithFullRows(t *testing.T, s *game.Session, numFullRows int) {
107		t.Helper()
108		if numFullRows > 0 {
109			fillRows(s.Board, numFullRows)
110		}
111	
112		// Spawn origin: O-piece occupies rows (origin.Row, origin.Row+1).
113		// Place it so its bottom (origin.Row+1) is one row above the filled zone.
114		// Filled rows start at BoardRows-numFullRows. O-piece bottom = BoardRows-numFullRows-1.
115		// So origin.Row = BoardRows-numFullRows-2.
116		var originRow int
117		if numFullRows == 0 {
118			// No full rows: just need a lockable position. Use ghost on empty board.
119			ap := &game.ActivePiece{
120				Kind:     game.KindO,
121				Origin:   game.Position{Col: 0, Row: 0},
122				Rotation: game.RotationNorth,
123			}
124			ghost := ap.GhostOrigin(s.Board)
125			originRow = ghost.Row
126		} else {
127			originRow = game.BoardRows - numFullRows - 2
128		}
129	
130		s.ActivePiece = &game.ActivePiece{
131			Kind:     game.KindO,
132			Origin:   game.Position{Col: 0, Row: originRow},
133			Rotation: game.RotationNorth,
134		}
135	
136		if !s.ActivePiece.AtRest(s.Board) {
137			t.Errorf("board setup: O-piece at row %d is not at rest — placement is wrong", originRow)
138			return
139		}
140	
141		s.LockTicksRemaining = 1
142	}
143	
144	// --- Scenario lines_and_score-1: Clearing rows awards score scaled by clear size and level ---
145	// Scenario index 0, 6 examples
146	
147	func TestACC_LinesAndScore1_ScoreScalesByClearSizeAndLevel(t *testing.T) {
148		defaults := []struct {
149			rows, level, linesBefore, scoreBefore, scoreAfter int
150		}{
151			{1, 1, 0, 0, 100},
152			{2, 1, 0, 0, 300},
153			{3, 1, 0, 0, 500},
154			{4, 1, 0, 0, 800},
155			{1, 3, 0, 50, 350},
156			{4, 5, 0, 0, 4000},
157		}
158		for i, def := range defaults {
159			rows := exInt(0, i, "rows", def.rows)
160			level := exInt(0, i, "level", def.level)
161			linesBefore := exInt(0, i, "lines_before", def.linesBefore)
162			scoreBefore := exInt(0, i, "score_before", def.scoreBefore)
163			scoreAfter := exInt(0, i, "score_after", def.scoreAfter)
164	
165			t.Run(strconv.Itoa(i), func(t *testing.T) {
166				s := sessionSetup(t, level, linesBefore)
167				s.Score = scoreBefore
168				setupLockWithFullRows(t, s, rows)
169				if t.Failed() {
170					return
171				}
172	
173				s.Tick()
174	
175				if s.Score != scoreAfter {
176					t.Errorf("rows=%d level=%d: score = %d, want %d", rows, level, s.Score, scoreAfter)
177				}
178			})
179		}
180	}
181	
182	// --- Scenario lines_and_score-2: Lines cleared total accumulates by the number of rows cleared ---
183	// Scenario index 1, 3 examples
184	
185	func TestACC_LinesAndScore2_LinesAccumulate(t *testing.T) {
186		defaults := []struct {
187			rows, level, linesBefore, linesAfter int
188		}{
189			{1, 1, 0, 1},
190			{4, 1, 5, 9},
191			{2, 2, 18, 20},
192		}
193		for i, def := range defaults {
194			rows := exInt(1, i, "rows", def.rows)
195			level := exInt(1, i, "level", def.level)
196			linesBefore := exInt(1, i, "lines_before", def.linesBefore)
197			linesAfter := exInt(1, i, "lines_after", def.linesAfter)
198	
199			t.Run(strconv.Itoa(i), func(t *testing.T) {
200				s := sessionSetup(t, level, linesBefore)
201				setupLockWithFullRows(t, s, rows)
202				if t.Failed() {
203					return
204				}
205	
206				s.Tick()
207	
208				if s.LinesClearedTotal != linesAfter {
209					t.Errorf("rows=%d linesBefore=%d: lines_cleared_total = %d, want %d", rows, linesBefore, s.LinesClearedTotal, linesAfter)
210				}
211			})
212		}
213	}

215	// --- Scenario lines_and_score-3: Crossing a line threshold advances the level ---
216	// Scenario index 2, 4 examples
217	
218	func TestACC_LinesAndScore3_LevelAdvancesOnThreshold(t *testing.T) {
219		defaults := []struct {
220			rows, level, linesBefore, levelAfter int
221		}{
222			{1, 1, 8, 1},
223			{2, 1, 8, 2},
224			{4, 1, 16, 3},
225			{1, 2, 5, 2},
226		}
227		for i, def := range defaults {
228			rows := exInt(2, i, "rows", def.rows)
229			level := exInt(2, i, "level", def.level)
230			linesBefore := exInt(2, i, "lines_before", def.linesBefore)
231			levelAfter := exInt(2, i, "level_after", def.levelAfter)
232	
233			t.Run(strconv.Itoa(i), func(t *testing.T) {
234				s := sessionSetup(t, level, linesBefore)
235				setupLockWithFullRows(t, s, rows)
236				if t.Failed() {
237					return
238				}
239	
240				s.Tick()
241	
242				if s.Level != levelAfter {
243					t.Errorf("rows=%d level=%d linesBefore=%d: level = %d, want %d", rows, level, linesBefore, s.Level, levelAfter)
244				}
245			})
246		}
247	}
248	
249	// --- Scenario lines_and_score-4: A lock that completes no full rows leaves score, lines, and level unchanged ---
250	// Scenario index 3, 2 examples
251	
252	func TestACC_LinesAndScore4_NoFullRowsNoChange(t *testing.T) {
253		defaults := []struct {
254			rows, level, linesBefore, scoreBefore int
255		}{
256			{0, 1, 0, 0},
257			{0, 4, 27, 9000},
258		}

261			level := exInt(3, i, "level", def.level)

263			scoreBefore := exInt(3, i, "score_before", def.scoreBefore)
264	
265			t.Run(strconv.Itoa(i), func(t *testing.T) {

273				s.Tick()
274	
275				if s.Score != scoreBefore {
276					t.Errorf("no-clear: score = %d, want %d (unchanged)", s.Score, scoreBefore)
277				}
278				if s.LinesClearedTotal != linesBefore {
279					t.Errorf("no-clear: lines_cleared_total = %d, want %d (unchanged)", s.LinesClearedTotal, linesBefore)
280				}
281				if s.Level != level {
282					t.Errorf("no-clear: level = %d, want %d (unchanged)", s.Level, level)
283				}
284			})
285		}
286	}

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/gabadi/workspace/gamaleds/tetris/.worktrees/architect/pkg/game/arch_test.go",
  "content": "package game_test\n\nimport (\n\t\"go/build\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"testing\"\n)\n\n// TestArchNoCycleDependency verifies that pkg/game does not import pkg/tui.\n// This enforces the dependency direction: tui → game, never game → tui.\nfunc TestArchNoCycleDependency(t *testing.T) {\n\tpkg, err := build.ImportDir(\".\", 0)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Skipf(\"cannot inspect package: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tfor _, imp := range pkg.Imports {\n\t\tif strings.Contains(imp, \"pkg/tui\") {\n\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"pkg/game must not import pkg/tui, found: %s\", imp)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n"
}

Lost the pre-work sanity check. Proceeded carefully on own judgment.\n- **Fixture design had incomplete scope on first pass**: Added `session_fixture.go` but missed `LockTicksRemaining`, `Bag.Remaining()`, and `Bag.DrainForTest()` initially, requiring a second edit pass. Root cause: scanned grep output but didn't catch all field names in the initial scope analysis.\n- **`arch_test.go` uses `build.ImportDir(\".\", 0)` which skips test files**: The test uses stdlib `go/build` which inspects non-test imports only. This is actually correct behavior (game test files can import more), but it's a subtle limitation that should be documented.\n- **`s.HoldUsed = true` workaround**: Original test set `s.HoldUsed = true` directly to simulate hold-used state, but I replaced it with `s.PlayerHolds()` which correctly exercises the state machine. This was the right call but added a subtle semantic difference — `PlayerHolds()` also consumes the active piece and spawns next. Verified it still tests the intended invariant (HoldUsed resets on spawn).\n\n## Actions\n| # | Type | Description | Target |\n|---|------|-------------|--------|\n| 1 | acknowledge | Exploration agent used proactively before code changes — good pattern | — |\n| 2 | acknowledge | Incremental build-and-test loop after each file prevented error cascade | — |\n| 3 | memory-update | Architect role: Session fixture boundary pattern established. ForTest methods are the approved injection interface for external test packages. Session fields remain public (same-package white-box tests still use them directly) | project memory |\n| 4 | rule-update | Fixture scope analysis: when scanning for direct field accesses, grep for ALL field names in one pass (including nested like LockTicksRemaining, Bag) rather than iterating — prevents second-pass edits | architect role prompt or personal feedback |\n| 5 | investigate | `arch_test.go` dependency check: `build.ImportDir` reads non-test Go files only. Works for production code direction. Test files in `game_test` package could import tui without being caught. {
  "file_path": "/Users/gabadi/.claude/projects/-Users-gabadi-workspace-gamaleds-tetris/memory/feedback_session_fixture_boundary.md",
  "content": "---\nname: feedback-session-fixture-boundary\ndescription: Session.ForTest methods are the approved test injection interface; acceptance tests must not access Session fields directly; arch_test.go enforces dependency direction\nmetadata:\n  type: feedback\n---\n\nAcceptance tests in external packages (acceptance/*) must not access `Session` struct fields directly. Use `ForTest` methods from `session_fixture.go` instead.\n\n**Why:** Direct field access from external packages creates tight coupling to Session's internal layout. If fields are ever made private, all external tests break. ForTest methods provide a stable API layer. Established during the lines-and-score architect pass (2026-06-10).\n\n**How to apply:**\n- For state injection: `s.SetActivePieceForTest(kind, col, row, rot)`, `s.SetScoreForTest(n)`, `s.SetLevelForTest(level, lines)`, `s.SetBoardCellForTest(col, row, kind)`, `s.SetLockTicksRemainingForTest(n)`, `s.ClearActivePieceForTest()`, `s.DrainBagForTest()`\n- For state read: `s.ActiveOriginForTest()`, `s.ActiveRotationForTest()`, `s.ActiveKindForTest()`, `s.ActivePieceNilForTest()`, `s.ScoreForTest()`, `s.LevelForTest()`, `s.LinesClearedTotalForTest()`, `s.LockTicksRemainingForTest()`, `s.BagRemainingForTest()`, `s.BoardCellForTest(col, row)`, plus `s.Snapshot()` for read-only snapshots and `s.CurrentPhase()` for phase queries\n- White-box tests in `package game` (same package) may still read/write fields directly — that's intentional\n- `pkg/game/arch_test.go` enforces `pkg/game` never imports `pkg/tui` via `go/build.ImportDir` (non-test files only)\n- When scanning for direct field accesses to replace, grep for ALL Session field names in one pass: `Phase Board ActivePiece Bag Score Level LinesClearedTotal HeldKind HoldUsed NextKind LockTicksRemaining FlashRows`\n"
}

1	# Memory Index
2	
3	- [Specifier run status](project_specifier_run_status.md) — cross-run ledger of which Tetris delivery to spec next; impl already done for all 8, specs missing for D4-D8
4	- [Specifier workflow](feedback_specifier_workflow.md) — auto mode, one feature at a time, six phases, advance only on QA completion notification
5	- [Go enum zero value](feedback_go_enum_zero_value.md) — put None/sentinel first in iota enums so zero-initialized arrays are empty, not filled
6	- [Index arithmetic tests](feedback_index_arithmetic_tests.md) — trace a concrete example before writing want= assertions for index/offset algorithms
7	- [mutate4go differential mode](feedback_mutate4go_differential.md) — --since-last-run uses function source hashes; test-only changes don't trigger re-run; delete manifest to force full run
8	- [Bag Peek/Draw fix pattern](feedback_bag_peek_draw.md) — guard Draw() with !Bag.Empty() in else branch; trace DrainForTest acceptance scenario before writing fix
9	- [crap4go empty output](feedback_crap4go_empty_output.md) — empty CRAP Report means all functions ≤6 (passing), not a tool failure
10	- [SwarmForge pipeline order](project_swarmforge_pipeline_order.md) — ux-engineer should run before cleaner/architect so arch sees full codebase before drawing boundaries
11	- [Git commit signing in swarmforge](feedback_git_commit_signing.md) — use --no-gpg-sign in worktree sessions; 1Password SSH agent fails intermittently
12	- [Hardening: skip vs fail in acceptance tests](feedback_hardening_skip_vs_fail.md) — use t.Errorf+return (not t.Skipf) for board setup mismatches; skips let Gherkin mutations survive
13	- [Mutation: pure data files get 0 coverage](feedback_mutation_data_files.md) — var table literals show 0 covered sites in mutate4go; skip full runs, data is validated via property/acceptance tests
14	- [CRAP/coverage investigation](feedback_crap_coverage_investigation.md) — go install fails ("does not contain package"); use `which crap4go` to check; empty report = all ≤6 (passing)
15	- [Integrator: no user interaction](feedback_integrator_no_user_interaction.md) — integrator must run full landing lifecycle autonomously; any human interaction outside specifier scope is a pipeline failure
16	- [SRS rotation property tests](feedback_srs_property_tests.md) — CW→CCW round-trip doesn't hold for SRS; use "successful rotation → valid placement" property instead
17	- [QA worktree setup](feedback_qa_worktree_setup.md) — run `mkdir -p tmp` before gherkin-parser or handoff file writes; tmp/ is not pre-created in worktrees
18	- [Integrator PR base branch](project_pr_base_branch.md) — feature PRs target `master`, not `entire/checkpoints/v1`; always use `--base master` explicitly
19	- [Integrator post-merge verify](feedback_integrator_postmerge_verify.md) — run tests from feature branch worktree before switching back; don't checkout origin/master into main project dir
20	- [Property tests run with suite](feedback_property_tests_run_with_suite.md) — go test ./... already covers property tests in pkg/game; no separate -run TestProperty needed unless isolating
21	- [Observation harness tmux wrapper](feedback_observation_harness_tmux.md) — use `command tmux` in harness scripts to bypass cmux shell wrapper; bare `tmux` silently misbehaves
22	- [QA test deduplication](feedback_qa_test_deduplication.md) — check for existing `qa_<feature>_test.go` before adding to `qa_suite_test.go`; ux-engineer creates per-feature files
23	- [Harness PROJECT_ROOT path](feedback_harness_project_root_path.md) — `observation-harness/<feature>/` is 2 levels below project root: `$SCRIPT_DIR/../..`
24	- [UX: check model before trusting comments](feedback_ux_check_model_before_trusting_comments.md) — read types.go/session.go directly before accepting a placeholder comment saying a field is missing
25	- [CRAP formula](feedback_crap_formula.md) — CRAP = CC×(1-cov)²+CC; coverage alone never reduces below CC; extract to lower CC when CC > 6
26
